2026-10-26T11:10:13.3924321Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Log per-request Prefill/Decode stage timings, including KV transfer, on the 9120.51 configuration

Add SGLang's --enable-request-time-stats-logging to both roles through
the shared speculation argument array, so the srun lines, the worker.sh
heredoc and the submission drift checks stay unchanged. At completion,
every attention-TP-rank-0 scheduler logs one line per request. Prefill:
bootstrap, queue and forward durations, entry time, KV transfer speed and
size. Decode: prealloc, transfer, queue and forward durations.

Why: in standalone run 40273247322, 121 of the 129 SLA failures were TTFT
failures in the first 10 s of the profiling phase. AIPerf drains warmup and
then starts all 240 sessions at once. Prefill DP0 began one 4032-token
request per iteration immediately, so about 36 prefills finished within
1 s across 12 ranks, yet only 3 first tokens reached the client by 1 s and
about 36 arrived together near 1.56 s. Logs cannot tell whether the KV
transfer, Decode bootstrap/preallocation of 240 requests, or Decode
admission holds them. In steady state, the transfer cost (about 388 MB
per request over NIXL) is also unmeasured.

No serving, kernel or image change otherwise; this is the same tree as
the preceding profile commit plus one logging flag. The previous Actions
run 40310899466 was cancelled while queued, but its Slurm job 26380 had
already started and is being collected separately.
